- **otel-collector-builder**: Build custom OpenTelemetry Collector distributions with OCB (OpenTelemetry Collector Builder). Use when authoring or debugging a builder manifest (builder.yaml), choosing component and provider versions, building a collector that bundles a custom or out-of-distribution component, setting up CI or Docker builds of a distribution, or troubleshooting OCB build failures. Triggers on "ocb", "collector builder", "custom collector distribution", "builder manifest", "builder-config", and version-mismatch errors from OCB builds. ([ollygarden/opentelemetry-agent-skills](https://github.com/ollygarden/opentelemetry-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/otel-collector-builder) · ★99)
